---
title: "aws_lex_bot"
description: "`aws_lex_bot` creates a real Amazon Lex V2 conversational bot: its locales, intents, and IAM role."
---

Lex V2 is the only Lex generation CloudFormation supports; the original Lex V1 has no CloudFormation resource type of its own.

## Example

<Tabs>
  <Tab title="Go">
    ```go
    package main

    import (
    	lex "github.com/ubiquex/ubx-sdk-aws/sdk/go/v2/aws/lex"
    	ubx "github.com/ubiquex/ubx-sdk-go/runtime"
    )

    func main() {
    	ubx.Main(ubx.Stack("example", func() {
    		ubx.Intent(ubx.IntentInfo{Summary: "example own bot"})

    		ubx.Resource(lex.Bot, "example", lex.BotConfig{
    			DataPrivacy:             map[string]any{"child_directed": true},
    			IdleSessionTtlinSeconds: 7200,
    			Name:                    "example",
    			RoleArn:                 "arn:aws:iam::123456789012:policy/example",
    			AutoBuildBotLocales:     true,
    			BotFileS3Location:       map[string]any{"s3_bucket": "example", "s3_object_key": "example"},
    			BotLocales:              []map[string]any{{"audio_filler_settings": map[string]any{"audio_type": "example"}}},
    			BotMembers:              []map[string]any{{"bot_member_alias_id": "example"}},
    		})
    	}))
    }
    ```
  </Tab>
  <Tab title="TypeScript">
    ```typescript
    import { intent, resource, stack } from "@ubx/sdk";
    import { Bot } from "@ubx/sdk-aws/aws/lex/bot";

    export default stack("example", () => {
      intent({ summary: "example own bot" });

      resource(Bot, "example", {
        dataPrivacy: { childDirected: true },
        idleSessionTtlinSeconds: 7200,
        name: "example",
        roleArn: "arn:aws:iam::123456789012:policy/example",
        autoBuildBotLocales: true,
        botFileS3Location: { s3Bucket: "example", s3ObjectKey: "example" },
        botLocales: [{ audioFillerSettings: { audioType: "example" } }],
        botMembers: [{ botMemberAliasId: "example" }],
      });
    });
    ```
  </Tab>
  <Tab title="Python">
    ```python
    import ubx_sdk as ubx
    from ubx.aws.lex import Bot, BotConfig
    from ubx.aws.lex.bot import Bot_DataPrivacy, Bot_BotFileS3Location, Bot_BotLocales, Bot_BotLocales_AudioFillerSettings, Bot_BotMembers

    def describe():
        ubx.intent("example own bot")

        ubx.resource(Bot, "example", BotConfig(
            data_privacy=Bot_DataPrivacy(child_directed=True),
            idle_session_ttlin_seconds=7200,
            name="example",
            role_arn="arn:aws:iam::123456789012:policy/example",
            auto_build_bot_locales=True,
            bot_file_s3_location=Bot_BotFileS3Location(s3_bucket="example", s3_object_key="example"),
            bot_locales=[Bot_BotLocales(audio_filler_settings=Bot_BotLocales_AudioFillerSettings(audio_type="example"))],
            bot_members=[Bot_BotMembers(bot_member_alias_id="example")],
        ))

    if __name__ == "__main__":
        ubx.run("example", describe)
    ```
  </Tab>
  <Tab title="Markdown">
    ```
    Create aws_lex_bot called "example", with data privacy a set
    value, idle session ttlin seconds 7200, role arn
    arn:aws:iam::123456789012:policy/example, auto build bot locales
    true, bot file s3 location a set value, bot locales a set value,
    bot members a set value.
    ```
  </Tab>
</Tabs>

## Input properties

<ResponseField name="auto_build_bot_locales" type="bool">
  Optional.
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="bot_file_s3_location" type="object">
  Configuration for the Amazon S3 location of the bot definition file, specifying the S3 bucket and object key used to import the bot. Optional. **(AI-inferred)**
  <Expandable title="properties">
    <ResponseField name="s3_bucket" type="string" required>
      The name of the Amazon S3 bucket that contains the bot definition file referenced by this location. Required. **(AI-inferred)**
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="s3_object_key" type="string" required>
      The S3 object key (path) of the JSON file that defines the bot, used when the bot's definition is stored in an S3 bucket. Required. **(AI-inferred)**
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="s3_object_version" type="string">
      Specifies the version ID of the S3 object that contains the bot definition file, allowing retrieval of a specific version when S3 versioning is enabled on the bucket. Optional. **(AI-inferred)**
    </ResponseField>
  </Expandable>
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="bot_locales" type="list(object)">
  Defines the list of locale-specific configurations for the bot, each with its own language and country, intents, slot types, and localized prompt/voice settings. Optional. **(AI-inferred)**
  <Expandable title="properties">
    <ResponseField name="audio_filler_settings" type="object">
      Configures the bot locale's audio filler settings, including the maximum duration a filler audio can play while the bot waits for user input. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
      <Expandable title="properties">
        <ResponseField name="audio_type" type="string">
          Specifies the type of audio filler (SILENCE or NOISE) that Amazon Lex uses to manage gaps in speech within a bot locale. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="enabled" type="bool">
          Whether the bot locale uses audio filler, which plays brief audio during pauses to make conversations feel more natural. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="minimum_play_duration_in_milliseconds" type="number">
          Specifies the minimum length of time, in milliseconds, that audio filler (e.g., a beep or spoken filler like 'um') is played during a conversation pause, so that the filler is not interrupted too abruptly when the user resumes speaking. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="response_delivery_delay_in_milliseconds" type="number">
          The delay in milliseconds that Amazon Lex waits before delivering the audio filler response to the user in the specified bot locale, used to prevent premature audio interruption during silence or barge-in. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="start_delay_in_milliseconds" type="number">
          The number of milliseconds that Amazon Lex waits before playing audio filler in a bot locale when a user pauses during a conversation. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
        </ResponseField>
      </Expandable>
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="custom_vocabulary" type="object">
      Configures the custom vocabulary for the bot locale, containing a list of domain-specific phrases with optional weights that Amazon Lex uses to improve speech recognition accuracy. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
      <Expandable title="properties">
        <ResponseField name="custom_vocabulary_items" type="list(object)">
          Defines the list of custom vocabulary entries (each with a phrase and optional weight/display-as) for the bot locale's custom vocabulary, controlling how Amazon Lex V2 recognizes and renders specific words. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
          <Expandable title="properties">
            <ResponseField name="display_as" type="string">
              Specifies the alternate display text used in conversation transcripts for this custom vocabulary item, replacing the raw phrase for readability. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
            </ResponseField>
            <ResponseField name="phrase" type="string">
              The word or phrase to add to the custom vocabulary for a Lex V2 bot locale, enabling the service to recognize and handle this expression in speech recognition. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
            </ResponseField>
            <ResponseField name="weight" type="number">
              Sets the relative preference (1-3) that Amazon Lex gives to this custom vocabulary item when matching speech input against the phrase. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
            </ResponseField>
          </Expandable>
        </ResponseField>
      </Expandable>
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="description" type="string">
      Provides a descriptive note for a bot locale, identifying the language or regional variant (e.g., English (US)) for the bot's conversational experience. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="generative_aisettings" type="object">
      Defines the generative AI settings for the bot locale, including configuration for integrating an Amazon Bedrock agent and enabling runtime features such as slot resolution improvement. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
      <Expandable title="properties">
        <ResponseField name="buildtime_settings" type="object">
          Configures the generative AI assistant's behavior during the bot building process, including whether it can retrieve knowledge base answers and the prompt specification used. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
          <Expandable title="properties">
            <ResponseField name="descriptive_bot_builder_specification" type="object">
              Configures the descriptive bot builder feature for the locale's build-time settings, enabling you to control whether the feature is active and specify the Amazon Bedrock model ARN used to generate human-readable descriptions for the bot and its components. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
              <Expandable title="properties">
                <ResponseField name="bedrock_model_specification" type="object">
                  Specifies the Amazon Bedrock foundation model and its inference parameters that the descriptive bot builder uses to generate or improve bot descriptions. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                  <Expandable title="properties">
                    <ResponseField name="bedrock_guardrail_configuration" type="object">
                      This object configures the Amazon Bedrock guardrail (e.g., its ID and version) that is applied to the Bedrock model used by the descriptive bot builder within the bot locale's generative AI buildtime settings. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                      <Expandable title="properties">
                        <ResponseField name="bedrock_guardrail_identifier" type="string">
                          Specifies the Amazon Bedrock guardrail identifier (such as its ARN) that is applied to the model's responses during Lex bot build time. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                        </ResponseField>
                        <ResponseField name="bedrock_guardrail_version" type="string">
                          Specifies the version (e.g., '1' or 'DRAFT') of the Amazon Bedrock guardrail that the descriptive bot builder should use to filter the model's responses. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                        </ResponseField>
                      </Expandable>
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="bedrock_model_custom_prompt" type="string">
                      Specifies a custom prompt to use with the Bedrock model for the descriptive bot builder during build time, overriding the default prompt. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="bedrock_trace_status" type="string">
                      Determines whether trace reporting (enabled or disabled) is activated for the Amazon Bedrock model used by the descriptive bot builder within the Lex bot's generative AI settings. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="model_arn" type="string">
                      The ARN of the Amazon Bedrock foundation model used to generate descriptive bot builder responses during build-time settings in the Lex bot's generative AI configuration. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                  </Expandable>
                </ResponseField>
                <ResponseField name="enabled" type="bool">
                  Enables or disables the descriptive bot builder, which uses generative AI to automatically generate or refine the bot's description and conversation flow during build time for this locale. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                </ResponseField>
              </Expandable>
            </ResponseField>
            <ResponseField name="sample_utterance_generation_specification" type="object">
              Configures automatic generation of sample utterances during bot build time, including toggling generation on/off and setting the maximum number of sample utterances to generate per intent or slot type. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
              <Expandable title="properties">
                <ResponseField name="bedrock_model_specification" type="object">
                  Specifies the Amazon Bedrock model used to generate sample utterances for the bot locale during build time. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                  <Expandable title="properties">
                    <ResponseField name="bedrock_guardrail_configuration" type="object">
                      Defines the Amazon Bedrock guardrail (identifier and version) to apply when the specified Bedrock model generates sample utterances. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                      <Expandable title="properties">
                        <ResponseField name="bedrock_guardrail_identifier" type="string">
                          The Amazon Resource Name (ARN) of the Amazon Bedrock guardrail to apply to the Bedrock model used for generating sample utterances in the Lex bot's build-time generative AI settings. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                        </ResponseField>
                        <ResponseField name="bedrock_guardrail_version" type="string">
                          Specifies the version of the Amazon Bedrock guardrail to use when the Lex bot's generative AI sample utterance generation feature invokes the Bedrock model. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                        </ResponseField>
                      </Expandable>
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="bedrock_model_custom_prompt" type="string">
                      Specifies a custom prompt template to guide the Bedrock model when generating sample utterances for the bot locale during build-time. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="bedrock_trace_status" type="string">
                      bedrock_trace_status indicates whether trace logging is enabled (ENABLED) or disabled (DISABLED) for the Amazon Bedrock model calls used during sample utterance generation in the bot locale. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="model_arn" type="string">
                      Specifies the Amazon Bedrock model ARN used to generate sample utterances during bot build time for the given locale. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                  </Expandable>
                </ResponseField>
                <ResponseField name="enabled" type="bool">
                  When enabled, Amazon Lex automatically generates additional sample utterances for the bot locale during the build process to improve model understanding. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                </ResponseField>
              </Expandable>
            </ResponseField>
          </Expandable>
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="runtime_settings" type="object">
          Specifies the runtime configuration for the Lex bot locale's generative AI feature, including the Amazon Bedrock model and inference settings used at runtime. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
          <Expandable title="properties">
            <ResponseField name="nlu_improvement_specification" type="object">
              Defines the configuration for the locale's natural language understanding improvement feature, which uses generative AI to enhance the bot's comprehension and classification of user input. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
              <Expandable title="properties">
                <ResponseField name="assisted_nlu_mode" type="string">
                  Determines whether the bot locale uses generative AI assistance through Amazon Bedrock to improve intent classification, with values typically 'ENABLED' or 'DISABLED'. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                </ResponseField>
                <ResponseField name="enabled" type="bool">
                  Whether to enable the NLU improvement specification, which uses generative AI to improve natural language understanding for the bot locale. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                </ResponseField>
                <ResponseField name="intent_disambiguation_settings" type="object">
                  Configures intent disambiguation for the bot locale, enabling or disabling Amazon Lex's ability to select the most likely intent when user input could match multiple intents as part of its NLU improvement settings. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                  <Expandable title="properties">
                    <ResponseField name="custom_disambiguation_message" type="string">
                      Specifies a custom message that Amazon Lex displays to the user to clarify which intent they want to fulfill when the bot cannot confidently determine the user's intent. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="enabled" type="bool">
                      Enables or disables intent disambiguation for the bot locale, which lets the bot ask clarifying questions when user input could match multiple intents. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="max_disambiguation_intents" type="number">
                      Sets the maximum number of intents Amazon Lex can consider as candidates when performing intent disambiguation for the bot locale's NLU improvement configuration. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                  </Expandable>
                </ResponseField>
              </Expandable>
            </ResponseField>
            <ResponseField name="slot_resolution_improvement_specification" type="object">
              Configures whether Amazon Lex uses generative AI to improve slot resolution in the bot locale, and optionally defines the elicitation prompt (via ElicitationSpecification) used when the improved resolution cannot determine a slot value. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
              <Expandable title="properties">
                <ResponseField name="bedrock_model_specification" type="object">
                  Specifies the Amazon Bedrock model configuration, including the model ARN and related settings, used by the slot resolution improvement feature for this Lex bot locale. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                  <Expandable title="properties">
                    <ResponseField name="bedrock_guardrail_configuration" type="object">
                      Configures the Amazon Bedrock guardrail applied when the Bedrock model performs slot resolution improvement, specifying the guardrail identifier and version to use. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                      <Expandable title="properties">
                        <ResponseField name="bedrock_guardrail_identifier" type="string">
                          The ARN of the Amazon Bedrock guardrail to use with the Bedrock model for slot resolution improvement. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                        </ResponseField>
                        <ResponseField name="bedrock_guardrail_version" type="string">
                          The version of the Amazon Bedrock guardrail to apply when using the Bedrock model for slot resolution improvement, controlling the guardrail behavior for that model. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                        </ResponseField>
                      </Expandable>
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="bedrock_model_custom_prompt" type="string">
                      Custom prompt text passed to the Bedrock model when it is used to improve slot resolution in Lex bot conversations. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="bedrock_trace_status" type="string">
                      Specifies whether trace logging is enabled for the Amazon Bedrock model used in slot resolution improvement, with allowed values ENABLED and DISABLED. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="model_arn" type="string">
                      This is the ARN of the Amazon Bedrock model used by the slot resolution improvement specification to enhance slot filling in a bot locale's generative AI runtime settings for Amazon Lex. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                  </Expandable>
                </ResponseField>
                <ResponseField name="enabled" type="bool">
                  Enables the slot resolution improvement specification within the bot locale's generative AI runtime settings, which directs Amazon Lex to use advanced machine learning to enhance the accuracy of resolving slot values from user input. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                </ResponseField>
              </Expandable>
            </ResponseField>
          </Expandable>
        </ResponseField>
      </Expandable>
    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="intents" type="list(object)">
      Specifies the list of intents defined for a user locale in the Lex bot, where each intent includes its name, sample utterances, and slot configuration. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
      <Expandable title="properties">
        <ResponseField name="bedrock_agent_intent_configuration" type="object">
          Defines the Bedrock agent intent configuration for the intent, specifying the Bedrock agent and agent intent IDs that this Lex intent uses to delegate handling to a Bedrock agent. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
          <Expandable title="properties">
            <ResponseField name="bedrock_agent_configuration" type="object">
              Defines the configuration for the Amazon Bedrock agent that Lex should invoke for this intent, such as the Bedrock agent alias ARN. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
              <Expandable title="properties">
                <ResponseField name="bedrock_agent_alias_id" type="string">
                  The identifier of the Amazon Bedrock agent alias that this intent uses to invoke the agent for handling conversation. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                </ResponseField>
                <ResponseField name="bedrock_agent_id" type="string">
                  The unique identifier of the Amazon Bedrock agent that the Lex bot intent is configured to use for agent-based conversation handling. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                </ResponseField>
              </Expandable>
            </ResponseField>
            <ResponseField name="bedrock_agent_intent_knowledge_base_configuration" type="object">
              Configures the Amazon Bedrock knowledge base (identified by its ARN) that this Lex Bedrock agent intent will use to retrieve and ground its responses. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
              <Expandable title="properties">
                <ResponseField name="bedrock_knowledge_base_arn" type="string">
                  The ARN of the Amazon Bedrock knowledge base that the Bedrock agent for this intent uses to retrieve information for generating responses. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                </ResponseField>
                <ResponseField name="bedrock_model_configuration" type="object">
                  This object configures the Amazon Bedrock model used by the Bedrock agent intent's knowledge base, including the model ARN and related model parameters. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                  <Expandable title="properties">
                    <ResponseField name="bedrock_guardrail_configuration" type="object">
                      Specifies the Amazon Bedrock guardrail configuration (including the guardrail identifier and version) to apply to the Bedrock model used by the knowledge base configuration for the Lex bot intent. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                      <Expandable title="properties">
                        <ResponseField name="bedrock_guardrail_identifier" type="string">
                          The identifier (such as an ARN or name) of the Amazon Bedrock guardrail attached to the model configuration for the knowledge base, used to filter or control the model's responses for this intent. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                        </ResponseField>
                        <ResponseField name="bedrock_guardrail_version" type="string">
                          Specifies the version of the Amazon Bedrock guardrail used by the intent's model configuration, such as 'DRAFT' or a specific numeric version like '1'. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                        </ResponseField>
                      </Expandable>
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="bedrock_model_custom_prompt" type="string">
                      Specifies a custom prompt string used to guide the Bedrock model's behavior when it generates responses based on the knowledge base for this intent. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="bedrock_trace_status" type="string">
                      Controls whether detailed trace logs are generated for Bedrock model invocations used by the knowledge base in this Bedrock agent intent, with allowed values like ENABLED or DISABLED. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="model_arn" type="string">
                      The ARN of the Amazon Bedrock foundation model used by the knowledge base associated with the Bedrock agent intent configuration in the Lex bot. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                  </Expandable>
                </ResponseField>
              </Expandable>
            </ResponseField>
          </Expandable>
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="description" type="string">
          A human-readable description of the intent that clarifies its purpose within the bot locale. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="dialog_code_hook" type="object">
          Configures the dialog code hook for this intent, including whether it is enabled and the ARN of the Lambda function to invoke for dialog initialization and validation. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
          <Expandable title="properties">
            <ResponseField name="enabled" type="bool">
              When enabled, the dialog code hook (a Lambda function) is invoked for each user input in the intent's conversation to handle fulfillment and validation. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
            </ResponseField>
          </Expandable>
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="display_name" type="string">
          Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS.
        </ResponseField>
        <ResponseField name="fulfillment_code_hook" type="object">
          Configures the fulfillment code hook, including the Lambda function and optional post-fulfillment updates, that is invoked to complete the user's request once the intent is fully elicited. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
          <Expandable title="properties">
            <ResponseField name="enabled" type="bool">
              Indicates whether the fulfillment code hook is enabled for the intent, allowing an AWS Lambda function to be invoked to generate a response and fulfill the user's request. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
            </ResponseField>
            <ResponseField name="fulfillment_updates_specification" type="object">
              Configures the timeout and provides responses to the user while the fulfillment code hook is running, including start, update, and final messages, to keep the session active during async fulfillment. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
              <Expandable title="properties">
                <ResponseField name="active" type="bool">
                  Indicates whether fulfillment updates are enabled for the intent, controlling whether Lambda can send progress updates to the user during the fulfillment process. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                </ResponseField>
                <ResponseField name="start_response" type="object">
                  Defines the initial message and its configuration that Lex sends to the user when the fulfillment update starts, including the message groups and whether the response can be interrupted. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                  <Expandable title="properties">
                    <ResponseField name="allow_interrupt" type="bool">
                      Whether the user can interrupt the start response message while it is being played. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="delay_in_seconds" type="number">
                      Number of seconds to wait after the fulfillment code hook starts before sending the start response message to the user. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                    </ResponseField>
                    <ResponseField name="message_groups" type="list(object)">
                      Defines the message groups for the start response, which determine the initial message(s) sent to the user when the Lambda fulfillment code hook begins, where each message group can contain a message and its variations. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                      <Expandable title="properties">
                        <ResponseField name="message" type="object">
                          Defines a message variant (with contentType and content) that appears as part of a message group in the start response sent to the user when the fulfillment code hook begins executing its updates. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                          <Expandable title="properties">
                            <ResponseField name="custom_payload" type="object">
                              Specifies the custom payload, a free-form string, for the message that is sent to the user when the fulfillment code hook starts providing an update. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                              <Expandable title="properties">
                                <ResponseField name="value" type="string">
                                  Specifies the JSON-formatted custom payload string that is sent to the messaging platform for this start response message in the fulfillment code hook. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                                </ResponseField>
                              </Expandable>
                            </ResponseField>
                            <ResponseField name="image_response_card" type="object">
                              Defines the image response card message for the start response of the fulfillment update, specifying the card's title, subtitle, image URL, and optional buttons to present to the user. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                              <Expandable title="properties">
                                <ResponseField name="buttons" type="list(object)">
                                  Defines the list of buttons displayed on the image response card, where each button has a text label and a value sent back to the bot when selected. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                                  <Expandable title="properties">
                                    <Note>Nested `buttons` structure continues beyond 8 levels of depth; further properties omitted for readability.</Note>
                                  </Expandable>
                                </ResponseField>
                                <ResponseField name="image_url" type="string">
                                  The URL of the image displayed in the image response card included in the start response message for the fulfillment updates specification of a code hook. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
                                </ResponseField>
                                <ResponseField name="subtitle" type="string">
                                  The subtitle text displayed beneath the title in the image response card shown during the Lex bot's fulfillment update message. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**

  <Expandable title="properties">

    <ResponseField name="bot_member_name" type="string">

    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="bot_member_version" type="string">

  </Expandable>
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="bot_tags" type="list(object)">

  <Expandable title="properties">

    </ResponseField>
    <ResponseField name="value" type="string">
      Stores the value portion of a key-value tag assigned to the Amazon Lex bot for resource metadata and management. Optional; if omitted, computed by AWS. **(AI-inferred)**
    </ResponseField>
  </Expandable>
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="bot_type" type="string">
  The type of a bot to create. Optional.
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="data_privacy" type="object" required>
  Specifies data privacy settings for the bot, including the ChildDirected flag that indicates whether the bot is intended for children under 13, affecting how Amazon Lex stores and processes speech and user data. Required. **(AI-inferred)**
  <Expandable title="properties">
    <ResponseField name="child_directed" type="bool" required>
      Indicates whether the Amazon Lex bot collects information from children under age 13, requiring compliance with the Children's Online Privacy Protection Act (COPPA). Required. **(AI-inferred)**
    </ResponseField>
  </Expandable>
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="description" type="string">
  A description of the resource Optional.
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="error_log_settings" type="object">
  Configures the CloudWatch log group and IAM role used to store error logs generated by the Amazon Lex bot. Optional. **(AI-inferred)**
  <Expandable title="properties">
    <ResponseField name="enabled" type="bool" required>
      Toggles whether the Amazon Lex bot captures and forwards error-level conversation logs to the configured log destination. Required. **(AI-inferred)**
    </ResponseField>
  </Expandable>
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="idle_session_ttlin_seconds" type="number" required>
  Sets the number of seconds an Amazon Lex bot session remains active after the user's last interaction before the session is closed. Required. **(AI-inferred)**
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="name" type="string" required>
  The unique name assigned to the Amazon Lex bot, which is required and must be unique within the AWS account. Required. **(AI-inferred)**
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="replication" type="object">
  The 'replication' property defines the cross-region replication configuration for the Lex bot, specifying the IAM replication role and the list of destination AWS regions to which the bot is replicated. Optional. **(AI-inferred)**
  <Expandable title="properties">
    <ResponseField name="replica_regions" type="list(string)" required>

    </ResponseField>
  </Expandable>
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="role_arn" type="string" required>
  The ARN of an IAM role that Amazon Lex assumes to access other AWS services (such as Lambda functions) on behalf of the bot. Required. **(AI-inferred)**
</ResponseField>

                    </ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="test_bot_alias_tags" type="list(object)">

  </Expandable>
</ResponseField>

## Output properties

<ResponseField name="arn" type="string">
  The Amazon Resource Name (ARN) that uniquely identifies this Amazon Lex bot in AWS. Computed by AWS after `ship`. **(AI-inferred)**
</ResponseField>

<ResponseField name="id" type="string">
  The unique identifier of the Lex bot, which is the bot's name. Computed by AWS after `ship`. **(AI-inferred)**
</ResponseField>
